--- CBR-CDB ---
How much is patient 10038933 needed to pay for the cost of their hospital visit in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT SUM(cost.cost) FROM cost WHERE cost.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) = '2100' )
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'cost.cost'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'cost'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038933'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT SUM(cost.cost) FROM cost WHERE cost.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 ) AND strftime('%Y',cost.chargetime) = '2100'
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'cost.cost'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'cost'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038933'], ["strftime('%y',cost.chargetime)", '=', '2100']]}
----------

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of acetonuria survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acetonuria' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acetonuria'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acetonuria' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acetonuria'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------

List the top five most frequent procedures that patients underwent within the same month after being diagnosed with diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+1 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'diabetes mellitus without mention of complication, type ii or unspecified type, not stated as uncontrolled'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Can you tell me the number of patients that were in the psychiatry careunit since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT admissions.subject_id ) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT transfers.hadm_id FROM transfers WHERE transfers.careunit = 'psychiatry' AND datetime(transfers.intime) >= datetime(current_time,'-0 year') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'transfers.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'transfers'], 'cond': [['datetime(transfers.intime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-0 year')"], ['transfers.careunit', '=', 'psychiatry']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT admissions.subject_id ) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT transfers.hadm_id FROM transfers WHERE transfers.careunit = 'psychiatry' AND strftime('%Y',transfers.intime) >= '2100' )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'transfers.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'transfers'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',transfers.intime)", '>=', '2100'], ['transfers.careunit', '=', 'psychiatry']]}

When did patient 10029291 last have a fecal bag event on 03/06/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT chartevents.charttime FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10029291 ) ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'fecal bag' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d',chartevents.charttime) = '2100-03-06' ORDER BY chartevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.charttime', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10029291'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'fecal bag'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',chartevents.charttime)", '=', '2100-03-06']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT outputevents.charttime FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10029291 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'fecal bag' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime) = '2100-03-06' ORDER BY outputevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10029291'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'fecal bag'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime)", '=', '2100-03-06']]}

What is the name of the medication that patient 10036156 received two or more times in their last hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10036156 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug HAVING COUNT(prescriptions.drug) >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10036156']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT( prescriptions.starttime ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10036156 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime DESC LIMIT 1 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10036156'], ['t1.c1', '>=', '2']]}
----------

How much does a thyroxine (t4), free lab test cost?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT SUM(cost.cost) FROM cost WHERE cost.hadm_id IN ( SELECT labevents.hadm_id FROM labevents WHERE labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'thyroxine (t4), free' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': 'sum', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['d_labitems.label', '=', 'thyroxine (t4), free']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DISTINCT cost.cost FROM cost WHERE cost.event_type = 'labevents' AND cost.event_id IN ( SELECT labevents.row_id FROM labevents WHERE labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'thyroxine (t4), free' ) )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['cost.cost', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.row_id'], 'tab': ['cost', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['cost.event_type', '=', 'labevents'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'thyroxine (t4), free']]}

How many patients were prescribed the medicine metronidazole (flagyl) within 2 months after a diagnosis of urinary tract infection, site not specified since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'urinary tract infection, site not specified' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'metronidazole (flagyl)' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'urinary tract infection, site not specified'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'metronidazole (flagyl)'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------

What are the five most commonly tested specimens for patients that were diagnosed with unarmed fight or brawl previously within 2 months?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unarmed fight or brawl' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-2 month') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-2 month') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unarmed fight or brawl'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-2 month')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-2 month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc FROM ( SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unarmed fight or brawl' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unarmed fight or brawl'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

For patients who underwent percutaneous abdominal drainage this year, what were the top five most frequent procedures that followed in the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'percutaneous abdominal drainage' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time) ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'percutaneous abdominal drainage'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Among patients who were prescribed with potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns, what are the top four most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T2.drug FROM (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS rank
    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE prescriptions.subject_id IN (
        SELECT subject_id FROM prescriptions
        WHERE drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'
        AND datetime(starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    )
    AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T2
WHERE T2.rank <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t2.rank', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'potassium chl 40 meq / 1000 ml ns'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

    FROM prescriptions
    WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
        SELECT prescriptions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'phenytoin infatab'
    )
    AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'phenytoin infatab'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Was the calculated total co2 level of patient 10038933 last measured on the first hospital visit less than the second to last measurement on the first hospital visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    ( SELECT labevents.valuenum 
      FROM labevents 
      WHERE labevents.hadm_id IN 
          ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
            FROM admissions 
            WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 
              AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
            ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
            LIMIT 1 ) 
        AND labevents.itemid IN 
          ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid 
            FROM d_labitems 
            WHERE d_labitems.label = 'total co2' ) 
      ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC 
      LIMIT 1 ) 
    < 
    ( SELECT labevents.valuenum 
      FROM labevents 
      WHERE labevents.hadm_id IN 
          ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
            FROM admissions 
            WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 
              AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
            ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
            LIMIT 1 ) 
        AND labevents.itemid IN 
          ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid 
            FROM d_labitems 
            WHERE d_labitems.label = 'total co2' ) 
      ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC 
      LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038933'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'total co2']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT labevents.valuenum FROM labevents WHERE labevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'calculated total co2' ) ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1 ) < ( SELECT labevents.valuenum FROM labevents WHERE labevents.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10038933 AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND labevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_labitems.itemid FROM d_labitems WHERE d_labitems.label = 'calculated total co2' ) ORDER BY labevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1 OFFSET 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_labitems.itemid', 'labevents.valuenum'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_labitems', 'labevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.dischtime', 'is', 'not null'], ['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10038933'], ['d_labitems.label', '=', 'calculated total co2']]}

Throughout this year, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed during the same hospital encounter to f patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 
        AND admissions.subject_id IN (
            SELECT patients.subject_id 
            FROM patients 
            WHERE patients.gender = 'f'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T2 
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id 
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'epilepsy, unspecified, not intractable, without status epilepticus'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What are the top five most frequent medications prescribed within 2 months to the patients in their 50s after they were diagnosed with cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'cerebral artery occlusion, unspecified with cerebral infarction'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the 6-month survival probability for patients living with a other and unspecified angina pectoris diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified angina pectoris' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified angina pectoris' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with other psoriasis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other psoriasis'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other psoriasis' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other psoriasis'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Could you tell me the name of the drug that was prescribed two or more times in 07/this year to patient 10020740?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT prescriptions.drug FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740 ) AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',current_time,'start of year') AND prescriptions.starttime >= datetime(current_time,'start of year') AND prescriptions.starttime < datetime(current_time,'start of year','+1 year') GROUP BY prescriptions.drug HAVING COUNT(*) >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ['prescriptions.starttime', '<', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','+1 year') ) >= 2"], ['prescriptions.starttime', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m','2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT( prescriptions.starttime ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740 ) AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') AND strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime) = '07' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 >= 2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["strftime('%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '07'], ['t1.c1', '>=', '2']]}

How many patients received albuterol inhaler in the same month after the confirmation of toxic encephalopathy?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'toxic encephalopathy'
    )
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'albuterol inhaler'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'toxic encephalopathy'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'albuterol inhaler'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'toxic encephalopathy' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'albuterol inhaler' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'toxic encephalopathy'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'albuterol inhaler'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------

Among patients who received partial hepatectomy since 1 year ago, what are the top three most common diagnoses that followed afterwards within 2 months?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'partial hepatectomy' ) AND datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'partial hepatectomy'], ['datetime(procedures_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

TRUTH:

Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'partial hepatectomy'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(procedures_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

Calculate the total number of patients diagnosed with dependence on renal dialysis who didn't revisit the hospital during the same month in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'dependence on renal dialysis'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100-01'
) 
AND admissions.subject_id NOT IN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', admissions.admittime) = '2100-01' 
    AND admissions.hadm_id NOT IN (
        SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100-01'
    )
)

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with irritable bowel syndrome but didn't come back to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, admissions.admittime 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE strftime('%Y', admissions.admittime) >= '2100'
) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE datetime(T2.admittime) > datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') OR T2.admittime IS NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.admittime', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'irritable bowel syndrome'], ['datetime(t2.admittime)', '>', "datetime(t1.charttime, '+2 month') or t2.admittime is null"], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'irritable bowel syndrome' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'irritable bowel syndrome'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

What are the five commonly ordered medications for patients aged 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
    ) AS T2
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many patients since 2100 underwent radical excision of other lymph nodes within the same hospital visit after other repair and plastic operations on bronchus?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) 
FROM ( 
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( 
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'other repair and plastic operations on bronchus' 
    ) 
    AND datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01') 
) AS T1 
JOIN ( 
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( 
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'radical excision of other lymph nodes' 
    ) 
    AND datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01') 
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'other repair'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'radical excision of other lymph nodes'], ['datetime(procedures_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id']]}
----------

Since 1 year ago, what are the top five most common drugs prescribed in the same month to m patients aged 50s after being diagnosed with unspecified sinusitis (chronic)?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified sinusitis (chronic)'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Count the number of patients who had arterial catheterization in the same month after having been diagnosed with osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) 
FROM ( 
  SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
  FROM diagnoses_icd 
  JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( 
    SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
    FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
    WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh' 
  ) 
  AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
) AS T1 
JOIN ( 
  SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
  FROM procedures_icd 
  JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
  WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( 
    SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
    FROM d_icd_procedures 
    WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' 
  ) 
  AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' 
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND strftime('%Y-%m',T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',T2.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'arterial catheterization'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',t2.charttime)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'osteoarthrosis, localized, not specified whether primary or secondary, pelvic region and thigh' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'arterial catheterization' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month')

Among the patients who were prescribed fentanyl citrate, what are the five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'fentanyl citrate'
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.starttime = T2.starttime
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'fentanyl citrate'], ['t1.starttime', '=', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:

What is the 3-month survival rate for patients diagnosed with history of falling?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'history of falling' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'history of falling'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

Among patients in their 30s since 2100, what are the top three prescribed drugs?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM (
  SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
  FROM prescriptions
  JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
  WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39
  AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
  GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 ) AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}

This year, what are the top five most frequently prescribed medications for patients who were also prescribed alteplase at the same time?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( 
  SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
  FROM prescriptions 
  WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') 
    AND prescriptions.subject_id IN ( 
      SELECT subject_id FROM prescriptions AS p2 
      WHERE p2.drug = 'alteplase' 
        AND datetime(p2.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') 
    ) 
  GROUP BY prescriptions.drug 
) AS T1 
WHERE T1.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(p2.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['p2.drug', '=', 'alteplase'], ['t1.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'alteplase' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'alteplase'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the monthly minimum volume of void that patient 10014354 had since 10/06/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT MIN(monthly_volume) FROM (
  SELECT SUM(outputevents.value) AS monthly_volume, strftime('%Y-%m', outputevents.charttime) AS month
  FROM outputevents
  WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN (
    SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN (
      SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10014354
    )
  )
  AND outputevents.itemid IN (
    SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'void' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents'
  )
  AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d', outputevents.charttime) >= '2100-10-06'
  GROUP BY month
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.charttime', 'outputevents.value'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10014354'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'void'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime)", '>=', '2100-10-06']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT MIN(outputevents.value) FROM outputevents WHERE outputevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10014354 ) ) AND outputevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'void' AND d_items.linksto = 'outputevents' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime) >= '2100-10-06' GROUP BY strftime('%Y-%m',outputevents.charttime)
Flattened: {'sel': 'min', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id', 'outputevents.value'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_items', 'icustays', 'outputevents'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10014354'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'void'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'outputevents'], ["strftime('%y-%m-%d',outputevents.charttime)", '>=', "2100-10-06' )"]]}
----------

Give a list of the four most frequent medications that patients were prescribed during the same hospital encounter after being diagnosed with personal history of colonic polyps since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of colonic polyps'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-0 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4

What are the three frequently ordered medications for patients in their 20s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT prescriptions.drug
        FROM prescriptions
        WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
            SELECT admissions.hadm_id
            FROM admissions
            WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
        )
    ) AS T2
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 't1.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['t1.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------

What proportion of patients with a diagnosis of spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication survive over a 6-month period?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM patients JOIN diagnoses_icd ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/2 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/2 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'spinal stenosis, lumbar region, with neurogenic claudication'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/2 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}
----------

What are the five commonly prescribed drugs for patients diagnosed with personal history of malignant neoplasm of large intestine previously within 2 months, since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of malignant neoplasm of large intestine'
        )
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5

How many patients died during the same month after having been diagnosed since 1 year ago with acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
) 
AND strftime('%Y-%m', patients.dod) = strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) 
AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y-%m',patients.dod)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure'], ['datetime(patients.dod)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t3.dod,'start of month')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with posttraumatic stress disorder within 2 months throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN diagnoses_icd ON admissions.hadm_id = diagnoses_icd.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder') AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time) AND admissions.subject_id IN (SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 2 * 30)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'patients.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '<', '2 * 30'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'posttraumatic stress disorder' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE ( datetime(T3.dod) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') ) OR ( datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T3.dod) AND datetime(T3.dod,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'posttraumatic stress disorder'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null']]}

Among patients in their 60 or above , what are the top three prescribed drugs?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T2.drug FROM (
  SELECT prescriptions.drug, COUNT(*) AS C1
  FROM prescriptions
  JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
  WHERE admissions.age >= 60
  GROUP BY prescriptions.drug
  ORDER BY C1 DESC
  LIMIT 3
) AS T2
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions.drug', 't2.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.age >= 60 ) GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3

Since 1 year ago, what are the top four most commonly taken specimens for patients within 2 months after the diagnosis of encounter for immunization?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.spec_type_desc 
FROM (
    SELECT T2.spec_type_desc, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'encounter for immunization'
        ) 
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc, microbiologyevents.charttime 
        FROM microbiologyevents 
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
    AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') 
    GROUP BY T2.spec_type_desc
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.spec_type_desc', 't2.spec_type_desc', 't3.spec_type_desc'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'microbiologyevents'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'encounter for immunization'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['datetime(microbiologyevents.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
