How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness'
    ) 
    AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year')
) AS T1 
JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = T1.subject_id 
WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL 
AND strftime('%Y',patients.dod) = strftime('%Y',current_time) 
AND T1.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y',patients.dod)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 'admissions.hadm_id']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 'patients.subject_id', 't1.charttime', 't1.hadm_id', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'facial weakness'], ["datetime(patients.dod,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ['t2.hadm_id', '=', 't3.hadm_id']]}

Is respiratory rate of patient 10000032 last measured on the first ICU visit greater than the first value measured on the first ICU visit?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
      FROM chartevents 
      WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN 
          ( SELECT icustays.stay_id 
            FROM icustays 
            WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN 
                ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
                  FROM admissions 
                  WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10000032 ) 
            ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC 
            LIMIT 1 ) 
      AND chartevents.itemid IN 
          ( SELECT d_items.itemid 
            FROM d_items 
            WHERE d_items.label = 'respiratory rate' 
            AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) 
      ORDER BY chartevents.charttime DESC 
      LIMIT 1 ) 
    > 
    ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
      FROM chartevents 
      WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN 
          ( SELECT icustays.stay_id 
            FROM icustays 
            WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN 
                ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
                  FROM admissions 
                  WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10000032 ) 
            ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC 
            LIMIT 1 ) 
      AND chartevents.itemid IN 
          ( SELECT d_items.itemid 
            FROM d_items 
            WHERE d_items.label = 'respiratory rate' 
            AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) 
      ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC 
      LIMIT 1 )

----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10000032 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'respiratory rate' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime DESC LIMIT 1 ) > ( SELECT chartevents.valuenum FROM chartevents WHERE chartevents.stay_id IN ( SELECT icustays.stay_id FROM icustays WHERE icustays.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10000032 ) AND icustays.outtime IS NOT NULL ORDER BY icustays.intime ASC LIMIT 1 ) AND chartevents.itemid IN ( SELECT d_items.itemid FROM d_items WHERE d_items.label = 'respiratory rate' AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' ) ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC LIMIT 1 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'chartevents.valuenum', 'd_items.itemid', 'icustays.stay_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'chartevents', 'd_items', 'icustays'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10000032'], ['d_items.label', '=', 'respiratory rate'], ['d_items.linksto', '=', 'chartevents'], ['icustays.outtime', 'is', 'not null']]}

How many people died after being diagnosed with long term (current) use of opiate analgesic during the same month during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(current_time,'start of month','-1 year') ) AND admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y-%m',patients.dod) = strftime('%Y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime) )
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id', 'patients.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of month','-1 year')"], ['patients.dod', 'is', 'not null'], ["strftime('%y-%m',patients.dod)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime)"]]}

What was the name of drug patient 10020740 was prescribed in the same hospital visit after receiving a other laparoscopic umbilical herniorrhaphy procedure since 01/2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T2.drug FROM (
    SELECT procedures_icd.charttime, procedures_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'other laparoscopic umbilical herniorrhaphy'
    ) 
    AND procedures_icd.hadm_id IN (
        SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM admissions 
        WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740
    ) 
    AND datetime(procedures_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, prescriptions.hadm_id 
    FROM prescriptions 
    WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (
        SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM admissions 
        WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740
    ) 
    AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01')
) AS T2 
ON T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.starttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.hadm_id', 't2.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'prescriptions', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'other laparoscopic umbilical herniorrhaphy'], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['datetime(procedures_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-01-01')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T2.drug FROM ( SELECT procedures_icd.charttime, procedures_icd.hadm_id FROM procedures_icd WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'other laparoscopic umbilical herniorrhaphy' ) AND procedures_icd.hadm_id IN (SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740 ) AND strftime('%Y-%m',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100-01' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, prescriptions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.hadm_id IN (SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10020740 ) AND strftime('%Y-%m',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100-01' ) AS T2 ON T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.hadm_id', 'prescriptions.starttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.hadm_id', 't2.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'prescriptions', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['admissions.subject_id', '=', '10020740'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'other laparoscopic umbilical herniorrhaphy'], ["strftime('%y-%m',prescriptions.starttime)", '>=', '2100-01'], ["strftime('%y-%m',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100-01'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime']]}

List the top five most frequent procedures that patients underwent during the same month after being diagnosed with adverse effect of anticoagulants, initial encounter in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title 
FROM d_icd_procedures 
WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
                WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'adverse effect of anticoagulants, initial encounter'
            ) 
            AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
            FROM procedures_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100'
        ) AS T2 
        ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
        AND strftime('%Y-%m',T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m',T2.charttime) 
        GROUP BY T2.icd_code
    ) AS T3 
    WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
)
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'adverse effect of anticoagulants, initial encounter'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m',t2.charttime)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'adverse effect of anticoagulants, initial encounter' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.icd_code, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'adverse effect of anticoagulants, initial encounter'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma but didn't come back to the hospital within the same month.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma'
    )
) 
AND admissions.subject_id NOT IN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id 
    FROM admissions 
    WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', admissions.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m', (
        SELECT diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma'
        )
    ))
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma' ) ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'chronic hepatitis c without mention of hepatic coma'], ["datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month')"], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of alcoholic cirrhosis of liver within the same month?
----------

Count the number of patients who were diagnosed with hypospadias but didn't come back to the hospital within 2 months in 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
) AS T1 
LEFT JOIN admissions AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
AND datetime(T2.admittime) > datetime(T1.charttime) 
AND datetime(T2.admittime) <= datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month') 
WHERE T2.hadm_id IS NULL
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hypospadias'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.hadm_id', 'is', 'null']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hypospadias' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) = '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id', 't2.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hypospadias'], ["strftime('%y',admissions.admittime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t2.charttime', '<', 'admissions.admittime']]}

Among patients who were diagnosed with acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous during this year, what are the top five most commonly prescribed medications that followed in the same hospital encounter for patients in their 50s?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59
        AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'acute alcoholic intoxication in alcoholism, continuous'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

What is the 3-month survival probability for patients living with a asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status diagnosis?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT AVG(CASE WHEN strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = diagnoses_icd.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT AVG(C1) FROM ( SELECT ( CASE WHEN (strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',T1.charttime)) < 1 * 365/4 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status' ) AND strftime('%J',current_time) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= 1 * 365/4 GROUP BY admissions.subject_id HAVING MIN(diagnoses_icd.charttime) = diagnoses_icd.charttime ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON T1.subject_id = patients.subject_id )
Flattened: {'sel': 'avg', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.dod', 't1.charttime'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'asymptomatic human immunodeficiency virus [hiv] infection status'], ["strftime('%j','2100-12-31 23:59:00') - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '1 * 365/4 ) = diagnoses_icd.charttime']]}

What are the three most frequently ordered medications that f patients aged 60 or above were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime
        FROM diagnoses_icd
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        )
        AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE admissions.age >= 60
        AND strftime('%Y', prescriptions.starttime) = '2100'
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age >= 60 AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['admissions.age', '>=', '60'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of septic shock within 2 months during the last year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT diagnoses_icd.subject_id) FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN patients ON diagnoses_icd.subject_id = patients.subject_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'septic shock') AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', 'now', '-1 year') AND strftime('%J',patients.dod) - strftime('%J',diagnoses_icd.charttime) <= 2 * 30
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.subject_id'], 'tab': ['d_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'septic shock'], ["strftime('%j',patients.dod) - strftime('%j',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '<=', '2 * 30'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year')"]]}

Count the number of patients who had insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage within 2 months after having been diagnosed with home accidents since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'home accidents'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
    FROM procedures_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_procedures 
        WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'
    )
) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
AND strftime('%J', T2.charttime) - strftime('%J', T1.charttime) <= 2 * 30
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'home accidents'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'], ["strftime('%j',t2.charttime) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '<=', '2 * 30'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'home accidents' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage' ) AND strftime('%Y',procedures_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't1.subject_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'd_icd_procedures', 'diagnoses_icd', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'home accidents'], ['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'insertion of intercostal catheter for drainage'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y',procedures_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime']]}

What were the five most commonly given microbiology tests for patients who had received excision of right internal mammary artery, percutaneous endoscopic approach before in the same month?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.test_name 
FROM (
    SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime 
        FROM procedures_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_procedures 
            WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'excision of right internal mammary artery, percutaneous endoscopic approach'
        )
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime 
        FROM microbiologyevents 
        JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
    ) AS T2 
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
    AND strftime('%Y-%m', T1.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', T2.charttime) 
    GROUP BY T2.test_name
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'excision of right internal mammary artery, percutaneous endoscopic approach'], ["strftime('%y-%m',t1.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', t2.charttime)"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.test_name FROM ( SELECT T2.test_name, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, procedures_icd.charttime FROM procedures_icd JOIN admissions ON procedures_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE procedures_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_procedures.icd_code FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.long_title = 'excision of right internal mammary artery, percutaneous endoscopic approach' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, microbiologyevents.test_name, microbiologyevents.charttime FROM microbiologyevents JOIN admissions ON microbiologyevents.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.test_name ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_procedures.icd_code', 'microbiologyevents.charttime', 'microbiologyevents.test_name', 'procedures_icd.charttime', 't2.test_name', 't3.test_name'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_procedures', 'microbiologyevents', 'procedures_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_procedures.long_title', '=', 'excision of right internal mammary artery, percutaneous endoscopic approach'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.charttime,'start of month')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

Among patients who were diagnosed with unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma during this year, what were the top three most common diagnoses that followed within 2 months?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title 
FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
                WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'
            ) 
            AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y', current_time)
        ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
        AND strftime('%J', T2.charttime) - strftime('%J', T1.charttime) <= 60 
        GROUP BY T2.icd_code
    ) AS T3 
    WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
)
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'], ["strftime('%j',t2.charttime) - strftime('%j',t1.charttime)", '<=', '60'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y', '2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.long_title FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN ( SELECT T3.icd_code FROM ( SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime AND datetime(T2.charttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.icd_code ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3 )
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'd_icd_diagnoses.long_title', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'diagnoses_icd.icd_code', 't2.icd_code', 't3.icd_code'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified viral hepatitis c without hepatic coma'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.charttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to f patients in their 50s during the same hospital encounter after they were diagnosed with unspecified essential hypertension in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 
        AND admissions.subject_id IN (
            SELECT patients.subject_id 
            FROM patients 
            WHERE patients.gender = 'f'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T2 
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id 
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified essential hypertension' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 50 AND 59 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified essential hypertension'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t1.hadm_id', '=', 't2.hadm_id'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

Among patients who were diagnosed with asthma, unspecified type, unspecified since 1 year ago, what are the top three most frequent diagnoses that followed afterwards in the same month?
----------
PREDS:

FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code IN (
    SELECT T3.icd_code 
    FROM (
        SELECT T2.icd_code, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 
        FROM (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
                SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
                FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
                WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'asthma, unspecified type, unspecified'
            ) 
            AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
        ) AS T1 
        JOIN (
            SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.icd_code, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
            FROM diagnoses_icd 
            JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
            WHERE datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time, '-1 year')
        ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
        WHERE T1.charttime < T2.charttime 
        AND datetime(T1.charttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.charttime, 'start of month') 
        GROUP BY T2.icd_code
    ) AS T3 
    WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
)

  FROM diagnoses_icd 
  JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id

) AS T1 
JOIN 
( 
  SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
  FROM diagnoses_icd 
  JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id

What are the four most frequent drugs that were prescribed to m patients in their 30s during the same month after they were diagnosed with hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia during this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia'
        ) 
        AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 
        AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = strftime('%Y',current_time)
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') 
    GROUP BY T2.drug 
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ["strftime('%y',prescriptions.starttime)", '=', "strftime('%y','2100-12-31 23:59:00')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'hyposmolality and/or hyponatremia' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 30 AND 39 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'hyposmolality'], ["datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

What are the four most frequently ordered medications that m patients aged 20s were prescribed within 2 months after they were diagnosed with mitral valve disorders since 1 year ago?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders'
        ) 
        AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year')
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') 
    GROUP BY T2.drug 
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'mitral valve disorders' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'm' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime) >= datetime(current_time,'-1 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'mitral valve disorders'], ['datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['datetime(prescriptions.starttime)', '>=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'm'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '4']]}

Calculate the total number of patients diagnosed with other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use who didn't revisit the hospital within the same month since 2100.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) 
FROM admissions 
WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN (
    SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) 
AND admissions.hadm_id NOT IN (
    SELECT a1.hadm_id 
    FROM admissions a1 
    JOIN admissions a2 ON a1.subject_id = a2.subject_id 
    WHERE strftime('%Y-%m', a1.admittime) = strftime('%Y-%m', a2.admittime) 
    AND a1.hadm_id != a2.hadm_id
)
Flattened: {'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['a1.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.hadm_id'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd'], 'cond': [['a1.hadm_id', '!=', 'a2.hadm_id'], ['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'other'], ["strftime('%y',diagnoses_icd.charttime)", '>=', '2100'], ["strftime('%y-%m',a1.admittime)", '=', "strftime('%y-%m', a2.admittime)"]]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'other and unspecified anti-infectives causing adverse effects in therapeutic use' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(admissions.admittime,'start of month') )

What is the difference between platelet count last measured on the first hospital visit compared to the first value measured on the first hospital visit for patient 10009628?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT 
    ( 
        SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
        FROM chartevents 
        WHERE chartevents.hadm_id IN 
            ( 
                SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
                FROM admissions 
                WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10009628 
                AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
                ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
                LIMIT 1 
            ) 
        AND chartevents.itemid IN 
            ( 
                SELECT d_items.itemid 
                FROM d_items 
                WHERE d_items.label = 'platelet count' 
                AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' 
            ) 
        ORDER BY chartevents.charttime DESC 
        LIMIT 1 
    ) 
    - 
    ( 
        SELECT chartevents.valuenum 
        FROM chartevents 
        WHERE chartevents.hadm_id IN 
            ( 
                SELECT admissions.hadm_id 
                FROM admissions 
                WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10009628 
                AND admissions.dischtime IS NOT NULL 
                ORDER BY admissions.admittime ASC 
                LIMIT 1 
            ) 
        AND chartevents.itemid IN 
            ( 
                SELECT d_items.itemid 
                FROM d_items 
                WHERE d_items.label = 'platelet count' 
                AND d_items.linksto = 'chartevents' 
            ) 
        ORDER BY chartevents.charttime ASC 
        LIMIT 1 
    )

TRUTH:

What are the three most frequent drugs prescribed to f patients with an age of 20s within the same month after they were diagnosed with unspecified acquired hypothyroidism?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM diagnoses_icd 
        JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
            SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
            FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
            WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'
        ) 
        AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29
    ) AS T1 
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id 
        FROM prescriptions 
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
        WHERE admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 
        AND admissions.subject_id IN (
            SELECT patients.subject_id 
            FROM patients 
            WHERE patients.gender = 'f'
        )
    ) AS T2 
    ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
    WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
    AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') 
    GROUP BY T2.drug 
) AS T3 
WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.hadm_id', 'admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism' ) ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.gender = 'f' ) AND admissions.age BETWEEN 20 AND 29 ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime,'start of month') GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'd_icd_diagnoses.icd_code', 'diagnoses_icd.charttime', 'patients.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'd_icd_diagnoses', 'diagnoses_icd', 'patients', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['d_icd_diagnoses.long_title', '=', 'unspecified acquired hypothyroidism'], ["datetime(t1.charttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime,'start of month')"], ['patients.gender', '=', 'f'], ['t1.charttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '3']]}

Among the patients who were prescribed alprazolam, what are the five most frequently prescribed drugs at the same time in this year?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT T3.drug FROM (
    SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1
    FROM (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'alprazolam'
        AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T1
    JOIN (
        SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime
        FROM prescriptions
        JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
        WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime, 'start of year') = datetime(current_time, 'start of year', '-0 year')
    ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id
    WHERE T1.starttime < T2.starttime
    AND datetime(T1.starttime, 'start of month') = datetime(T2.starttime, 'start of month')
    GROUP BY T2.drug
) AS T3
WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', 'start of year', '-0 year')"], ["datetime(t1.starttime,'start of month')", '=', "datetime(t2.starttime, 'start of month')"], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'alprazolam'], ['t1.starttime', '<', 't2.starttime'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'alprazolam' AND datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year') = datetime(current_time,'start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 5
Flattened: {'sel': '', 'agg': ['admissions.subject_id', 'prescriptions.drug', 'prescriptions.starttime', 't2.drug', 't3.drug'], 'tab': ['admissions', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [["datetime(prescriptions.starttime,'start of year')", '=', "datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year')"], ['datetime(t1.starttime)', '=', 'datetime(t2.starttime)'], ['prescriptions.drug', '=', 'alprazolam'], ['t3.c1', '<=', '5']]}

How many patients were prescribed the medicine ferric gluconate within 2 months after a diagnosis of ankylosing spondylitis since 2100?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) 
FROM (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime 
    FROM diagnoses_icd 
    JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = (
        SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code 
        FROM d_icd_diagnoses 
        WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'ankylosing spondylitis'
    ) 
    AND strftime('%Y', diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100'
) AS T1 
JOIN (
    SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime 
    FROM prescriptions 
    JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id 
    WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'ferric gluconate'
) AS T2 
ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id 
WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime 
AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime, '+2 month')

Among those who were prescribed with pseudoephedrine, what are the three most habitually prescribed drugs at the same time in 2100?
PRED: SELECT T1.drug FROM ( SELECT prescriptions.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM prescriptions WHERE prescriptions.subject_id IN ( SELECT subject_id FROM prescriptions WHERE drug = 'pseudoephedrine' AND strftime('%Y',starttime) = '2100' ) AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' GROUP BY prescriptions.drug ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 <= 3
GOLD: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'pseudoephedrine' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE datetime(T1.starttime) = datetime(T2.starttime) GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 3
PRED_OUT: Counter({('bag',): 1, ('0.9% sodium chloride',): 1, ('sw',): 1, ('sodium chloride 0.9%  flush',): 1, ('ondansetron',): 1, ('levothyroxine sodium',): 1, ('lactated ringers',): 1, ('iso-osmotic dextrose',): 1})

What are the most common distribution routes for drugs prescribed to patients aged 65 or older?
PRED: SELECT prescriptions.route, COUNT(*) AS frequency FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE admissions.age >= 65 GROUP BY prescriptions.route ORDER BY frequency DESC

Give a list of the four most frequent medications that patients were prescribed during the same hospital encounter after being diagnosed with personal history of colonic polyps since 2100.
PRED: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER (ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of colonic polyps' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
GOLD: SELECT T3.drug FROM ( SELECT T2.drug, DENSE_RANK() OVER ( ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC ) AS C1 FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'personal history of colonic polyps' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.drug, prescriptions.starttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND T1.hadm_id = T2.hadm_id GROUP BY T2.drug ) AS T3 WHERE T3.C1 <= 4
PRED_OUT: Counter()
GOLD_OUT: Counter({('0.9% sodium chloride',): 1, ('metoprolol tartrate',): 1, ('sodium chloride 0.9%  flush',): 1, ('acetaminophen',): 1})

How many patients died during the same month after having been diagnosed since 1 year ago with acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00', '-1 year') ) AND strftime('%Y-%m', patients.dod) = strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) AND patients.dod IS NOT NULL
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'acute on chronic diastolic (congestive) heart failure' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod) >= datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')

GOLD: SELECT d_icd_procedures.long_title FROM d_icd_procedures WHERE d_icd_procedures.icd_code IN ( SELECT T1.icd_code FROM ( SELECT procedures_icd.icd_code, COUNT( procedures_icd.charttime ) AS C1 FROM procedures_icd WHERE procedures_icd.hadm_id IN ( SELECT admissions.hadm_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id = 10029291 ) AND datetime(procedures_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') AND strftime('%m',procedures_icd.charttime) = '03' GROUP BY procedures_icd.icd_code ) AS T1 WHERE T1.C1 >= 2 )

How many patients died after having been diagnosed with facial weakness during the same hospital encounter throughout this year?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT T1.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T1 JOIN patients ON patients.subject_id = T1.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y',patients.dod) = strftime('%Y','2100-12-31 23:59:00') AND T1.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime, T1.hadm_id FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime, admissions.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'facial weakness' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-0 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE T2.hadm_id = T3.hadm_id

How many people died after being diagnosed with long term (current) use of opiate analgesic during the same month during the last year?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime,'start of month') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of month','-1 year') ) AND admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND strftime('%Y-%m',patients.dod) = strftime('%Y-%m',diagnoses_icd.charttime) )
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'long term (current) use of opiate analgesic' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime AND datetime(T1.charttime,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year') ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL AND datetime(patients.dod,'start of year') = datetime('2100-12-31 23:59:00','start of year','-1 year') ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')

PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.dose_unit_rx = 'vial' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'secondary and unspecified malignant neoplasm of intra-abdominal lymph nodes' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) = '2100' ) AS T1 JOIN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, prescriptions.starttime FROM prescriptions JOIN admissions ON prescriptions.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE prescriptions.drug = 'vial' AND strftime('%Y',prescriptions.starttime) = '2100' ) AS T2 ON T1.subject_id = T2.subject_id WHERE T1.charttime < T2.starttime AND datetime(T2.starttime) BETWEEN datetime(T1.charttime) AND datetime(T1.charttime,'+2 month')

What is the number of patients who died after diagnosis of alcoholic cirrhosis of liver within the same month?
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) AND strftime('%Y-%m', diagnoses_icd.charttime) = strftime('%Y-%m', patients.dod) ) AND admissions.subject_id IN ( SELECT patients.subject_id FROM patients WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL )
GOLD: SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT T1.subject_id, T1.charttime FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'alcoholic cirrhosis of liver' ) ) AS T1 GROUP BY T1.subject_id HAVING MIN(T1.charttime) = T1.charttime ) AS T2 JOIN ( SELECT patients.subject_id, admissions.hadm_id, patients.dod FROM admissions join patients ON patients.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE patients.dod IS NOT NULL ) as T3 ON T2.subject_id = T3.subject_id WHERE datetime(T2.charttime,'start of month') = datetime(T3.dod,'start of month')

Count the number of patients diagnosed with gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis who did not come to the hospital within 2 months since 2100.
PRED: SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT admissions.subject_id) FROM admissions WHERE admissions.subject_id NOT IN ( SELECT admissions.subject_id FROM admissions WHERE admissions.hadm_id IN ( SELECT diagnoses_icd.hadm_id FROM diagnoses_icd WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= datetime('2100-01-01') AND datetime(admissions.admittime) < datetime(diagnoses_icd.charttime, '+2 month') ) )
GOLD: SELECT ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T1.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T1 ) - ( SELECT COUNT( DISTINCT T2.subject_id ) FROM ( SELECT admissions.subject_id, diagnoses_icd.charttime FROM diagnoses_icd JOIN admissions ON diagnoses_icd.hadm_id = admissions.hadm_id WHERE diagnoses_icd.icd_code = ( SELECT d_icd_diagnoses.icd_code FROM d_icd_diagnoses WHERE d_icd_diagnoses.long_title = 'gastro-esophageal reflux disease without esophagitis' ) AND strftime('%Y',diagnoses_icd.charttime) >= '2100' ) AS T2 JOIN admissions ON T2.subject_id = admissions.subject_id WHERE T2.charttime < admissions.admittime AND strftime('%Y',admissions.admittime) >= '2100' AND datetime(admissions.admittime) BETWEEN datetime(T2.charttime) AND datetime(T2.charttime,'+2 month') )
